# Task pictures: the list

Generated by `pnpm --filter web art:list` from the task content. Do not edit by hand. How to make and add pictures: [README.md](README.md).

387 pictures: 85 priority 1, 158 priority 2, 144 priority 3. 73 done.

## Priority 1

### ✅ `art-src/tasks/discover/cover.png`

Used for: My island: Discover tasks without their own picture

```text
A young Egyptian man standing on a small green island at sunrise, looking out at other islands across a calm lavender sea, curious and hopeful. Warm, painterly digital illustration in a soft semi-realistic storybook style, gentle golden light, clean and uncluttered. Set in Egypt today. Young Egyptian people aged 18 to 25, modest contemporary everyday or work clothes. Across this complete image set, make about two thirds of the main subjects men and one third women; use men by default unless a scene specifies otherwise. Keep scenes single-gender by default: a man alone or working with male colleagues, or a woman alone or with women colleagues. Do not stage casual mixed-gender groups, socializing, or close interaction. When a story truly needs both genders in a workplace, show a brief formal professional exchange with respectful space and no physical contact. Women may wear hijab where appropriate. Do not exclude women from any profession. Small accents of violet (#7025B5) and apricot (#FFA366) in clothes or objects; the rest in natural, warm colours. No readable text, letters, numbers, logos or signs anywhere. No watermark, no frame, no border. Square image. Keep the main subject in the middle; the top eighth stays calm (sky, wall or ceiling) because buttons sit there, and the bottom tenth will be covered.
```
